PROVENANCE & COVERAGE

The source ledger

Every imported observation retains its source, date, classification, and collection method.

99 / 99Chamber composition records
99 / 99Regular-election schedules
6,139Seats with imported district inputs
686Source observations
0Imported market contracts

A recent retrieval does not make an old observation fresh. Composition is generally stale after 90 days; polls after 21 days; markets after 3 days. Dates shown below are the observation dates.

682 of 686 observations shown.

Current references identify input provenance, not independent verification. Historical district margins remain valid only for matching maps; their age is shown separately from map validity.

Refresh adapters

Silver Bulletin

Automatic on-demand check

Dated public generic-ballot commentary. Uses the current unadjusted D−R margin only; ambiguous or unavailable updates are rejected.

Official Maine House roster

Automatic on-demand check

Current voting-member party counts, excluding tribal delegates. Changed counts retain their retrieval date; unchanged checks do not replace source dates.

NCSL

Automatic check with manual fallback

Checks the full 99-chamber composition table. Access restrictions or validation failures retain existing records. Election schedules require reviewed imports.

Official election authority

Validated JSON import

Complete contested district sets, candidate updates, holdover composition and historical results.

Ballotpedia

Manual observation

Review candidate list completeness; never infer an uncontested race solely from a missing name.

State Navigate / CNalysis

API credentials unavailable

Import published data with provenance. Treat related forecasts as one independence group.

Kalshi

Verified contract unavailable

Requires direct chamber relevance, observation timestamp, volume, spread, wording and settlement.
